| 146 | **BUG (product quality / honest numbers, planner share surface): the post-waitlist share path is subscription-hardcoded, number-wrong and link-less for TARGET/GROWTH plans.** `mixStats()` (planner.js ~285) resolved ANY `GOALS[]` entry with a numeric `target`, but that field means `$/mo` for a SUBSCRIPTION goal and the **item price** for a TARGET goal (`iphone` 1100, `watches` 400, `sneakers` 180, none carrying `isMonthly`) — so an iPhone was read as a $1,100/mo subscription and `foreverNumber()` turned it into ~$155k-$220k of capital the user never entered. The mix-seed effect (~1684) ran for EVERY archetype, so that bogus bundle existed from first render. WHAT SHIPPED, all in `test_audit_prescan.js` (+259/−1, **zero product-file lines**): `stripJsCommentsAndStrings()` gains regex-literal state via a new `isRegexPosition()` (previous-significant-emitted-char heuristic, `REGEX_PREV_PUNCT`/`REGEX_PREV_KEYWORDS`), honouring `\` escapes and `[`…`]` classes in which `/` does not terminate, then dropping the literal like a string; a frozen verbatim copy of 185's pre-fix scanner as `legacyStrip()` that the tests assert stays **silently green at 3** on the same mutation the fixed scanner counts **4** (without it the new tests could pass for reasons unrelated to the fix); `deriveTailMarker()` + a Leg B invariant that the artifact's own final executable line (`process.exit(unresolved ? 3 : 2)`, `audit-app.js:3201`, read at run time and asserted present in the raw source first so it cannot rot) survives stripping — one assertion that catches **any** future tail-swallowing shape, not only the two measured. **A BUG THE BUILD AGENT FOUND IN ITS OWN FIX MID-BUILD, kept because the reasoning is a fact about JavaScript rather than a hack:** `//` and `/*` must fall through to the comment branches *unconditionally* — an empty regex body and a bare leading `*` quantifier are both invalid JS, so neither can legally OPEN a regex; without that guard every `//` comment following a `;` or `}` parsed as an empty regex and corrupted the whole file. An unterminated regex bails at the newline, so a heuristic misfire costs at most one line and can never run to EOF — the exact failure mode this item removes. **A6b's assertion is byte-unchanged at `occurrences === 3`** — the count not moving is the evidence the gate did not get looser. DEVIATION, disclosed by the builder and **reproduced rather than accepted** by the verifier: the mutation fixtures inject before the tail marker, not "before the existing call sites" as criterion 2's prose says, because `audit-app.js`'s last literal `*/` is at offset 171,689 with all three real call sites at 156,628–170,081 — an earlier injection lets `legacyStrip` recover at that `*/` and return **4**, the same answer as the fixed scanner, making criterion 3 **unfalsifiable**. The honest consequence is stated, not glossed: "swallows the rest of the file" holds only when no `*/` follows; where one does, the damage is bounded to that span (which is exactly what `:324`/`:771`/`:811` do). NOT PROVEN, disclosed: not a real parser (post-increment-then-regex unhandled, absent from this repo); and the verifier's own sharpest finding — under a heuristic forced to treat every `/` as a regex start, the real file's count still came out 3 and only the explicit synthetic division test caught it, so the division direction's safety net is that test, not the real-file count. TESTS: `test_audit_prescan.js` **43/0 → 48/0** (A6b count still exactly 3), plain lane **39/39** exact baseline match, wall time **1m43.2s** inside the 5-min box, `git status --porcelain` clean after every mutation run (no stray fixtures, nothing under `tokens/`/`chains/`). | 187 | **The entire design system is held hostage by an unused third-party font CDN.** `style.css:1` is `@import url(https://api.fontshare.com/…satoshi…)`; every page loads `style.min.css` with the deferred pattern `media="print" onload="this.media='all'"`; a `<link>`'s `load` event does not fire until its `@import`ed sub-resources resolve — so when fontshare is blocked (ad-blocker, content blocker, corporate/regional filtering, CDN outage) the swap never runs and all **545 rules** of the design system stay applied to **print media only**. The page renders completely unstyled — no cards, no chips, no layout, no neumorphic tokens, no dark mode. Measured this run on **`/plan.html`, `/`, `/home.html?token=USDC` and `/home.html?pool=…` — 0 of 6 loads swapped**, `--color-background` empty on all of them, while `planner-styles.min.css`/`pool-detail-styles.min.css`/`landing-styles.css` (no `@import`) swapped every time. Cause proven by controlled substitution in a 5-line control file, not inferred: strip line 1 and the same sheet swaps to `all` and the token resolves `#E2E8F0`. **The imported font is never used** — `--font-family-base` (`style.css:93`) is `"FKGroteskNeue","Geist","Inter",…`, the real font is self-hosted at `style.css:783`, and `grep -i satoshi *.css` returns only the `@import` itself. So the request that can break every page fetches a typeface the product never renders; the fix is a deletion with no visual change on the happy path. Hits `?pool=` (the north-star surface), `?token=` (the SEO lifeline), `/plan.html` and `/`. Prod ships the identical markup (`curl` confirmed). Fix is a **2-line deletion** (`style.css:1` + its blank line) plus a script-regenerated `style.min.css` — the imported Satoshi face is referenced by **zero** `font-family` declarations, so there is no happy-path visual change; verifier programmatically confirmed the minified file’s post-`@import` content is byte-for-byte identical and that the committed artifact matches a fresh `node minify-assets.js` (md5 `836d9648…`).
